The judgment not only keeps track of the syntactic structure of types but also the
semantic information for both layers
d
and
m
. The semantic information for both layers is critical to enable code running, when we
refer to the code of types at layer
d
and
m
, resp..

The judgment for local substitutions is simple, which simply generalizes that for
terms:

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\cdot^{k}:\cdot}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}\cdot^{k}:\cdot}\par\frac{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\cdot_{g}^{k}:\cdot}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}\cdot_{g}^{k}:\cdot}\par\frac{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\textsf{wk}_{g}^{k}:g}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}\textsf{wk}_{g}^{k}:g}\par\frac{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}\delta:\Delta\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}t:T[\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\delta,t/x:\Delta,x:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}\delta,t/x:\Delta,x:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}

l_{1}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\vdash l_{2}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma,x:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}t:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$}\quad L\vdash l\approx l_{1}\sqcup l_{2}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T^{\prime}\approx\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\lambda^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).t:T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}\lambda^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).t:T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}\par\frac{L\vdash l_{1}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma,x:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}t:\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}\sqcup l_{2}$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}s:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\quad L\vdash l\approx l_{2}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T^{\prime}\approx T[s/x]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}(t:\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).T)~s:T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}(t:\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).T)~s:T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}

These rules strictly decrease on the structures of types, terms and local
substitutions. The purpose of these rules are to remember all computational and syntactic information
of all sub-structures. Notice that the annotated types do not necessarily match up with the precise types
which terms might have. For example,
zero
is quantified to have seemingly some arbitrary type
T
. This, however, is not an accurate understanding. In fact,
T
is quantified by
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\textsf{zero}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, so if we provide a proof of

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then we obtain
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\textsf{zero}:T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
by
irrelevance. More specifically,

Lemma 7.28 (Conversion).

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}t:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}t:T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

The rules are designed in this way so that there is a semantic structure which the
recursors can recurse on. Moreover, these rules are closed under weakenings and local substitutions, which is particularly
important for use of global variables.

Lemma 7.29 (Weakenings).

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
\alpha::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\Longrightarrow L\;|\;\Psi
,
then
L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}
,
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}t:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
\alpha::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\Longrightarrow L\;|\;\Psi
,
then
L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}t:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}
,
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}\delta:\Delta
and

\alpha::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\Longrightarrow L\;|\;\Psi
, then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}\delta:\Delta
.

Lemma 7.30 (Local Substitutions).

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}\delta:\Gamma
,
then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}T[\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}
,
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}t:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}\delta:\Gamma
,
then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}t[\delta]:T[\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}
,
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}\delta:\Delta
and

L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma
, then

L\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}\delta\circ\delta^{\prime}:\Delta
.

Proof.

We proceed by mutual induction. The syntax is already closed under local substitutions. The semantics is also closed under local substitutions by
Lemma

7.23
.
∎

We can lift from layer
v
to layer
c
.

Lemma 7.31 (Lifting).

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{v}}^{\textsc{d}}t:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}t:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{v}}^{\textsc{d}}\delta:\Delta
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}\delta:\Delta
.

Lemma 7.32 (Local Weakenings).

If
L\;|\;\Psi\vdash_{\textsc{d}}\Delta,\Gamma\approx\Delta^{\prime},\Gamma
and
L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\Delta\approx\Delta^{\prime}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta,\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}\textsf{wk}^{|\Gamma|}_{\Delta}:\Delta
.

In the semantic rules for global contexts and global substitutions, we use the
auxiliary definitions above. Similar to the counterparts for local contexts and local substitutions, the rules are
defined in an induction-recursion. The key idea is to make sure the relation is invariant under universe weakenings. We also impose invariance under global substitutions for related local contexts, types
and terms. We are going to define the following definitions:

•

\mathcal{D}::L\vDash\Psi\approx\Phi
denotes the semantic related global contexts.

•

L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}
denotes the semantic relation between the global
substitutions
\sigma
and
\sigma^{\prime}
. The two definitions above are defined inductive-recursively.

•

\mathcal{F}::L\;|\;\mathcal{E}\vDash_{p}^{p}\Gamma
given
\mathcal{E}::\forall~\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L~.~L\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\Phi
denotes
\Gamma
is semantically well-formedness and is stable under universe
weakening and related global substitutions.

•

L\;|\;\Psi;\mathcal{F}\vDash_{\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

denotes that
T
is semantically well-formedness and is
stable under universe weakening and related global substitutions.

We now move on to the actual definitions.

\mathcal{D}::\frac{}{L\vDash\cdot\approx\cdot}

L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}
is defined as
L\vdash\Psi
and
\sigma=\sigma^{\prime}=\cdot
.

\mathcal{D}::\frac{\mathcal{E}::\forall~\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L~.~L^{\prime}\vDash\Phi\approx\Phi^{\prime}}{L\vDash\Phi,g:\textsf{Ctx}\approx\Phi^{\prime},g:\textsf{Ctx}}

L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}
is defined as

•

\sigma=\sigma_{1},\Gamma/g
and
\sigma^{\prime}=\sigma_{1}^{\prime},\Gamma^{\prime}/g
,

•

\forall~\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L~.~L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma_{1}\approx\sigma_{1}^{\prime}:\mathcal{E}(\theta)
, and

•

L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\Gamma\approx\Gamma^{\prime}
.

\mathcal{D}::\frac{\mathcal{E}::\forall~\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L~.~L^{\prime}\vDash\Phi\approx\Phi^{\prime}\quad L\;|\;\mathcal{E}\vDash_{p}^{p}\Gamma\approx\Gamma^{\prime}\quad i\in\{\textsc{c},\textsc{d}\}}{L\vDash\Phi,U:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\approx\Phi^{\prime},U:(\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash_{i}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})}

L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}
is defined as

•

\sigma=\sigma_{1},T/U
and
\sigma^{\prime}=\sigma_{1}^{\prime},T^{\prime}/U
,

•

\forall~\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L~.~L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma_{1}\approx\sigma_{1}^{\prime}:\mathcal{E}(\theta)
,

•

We analyze
i
.

–

If
i=\textsc{c}
, then
T=T^{\prime}
,
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

–

Otherwise,
i=\textsc{d}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

where
L\;|\;\mathcal{E}\vDash_{p}^{p}\Gamma\approx\Delta
is defined as

\forall~~\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L\text{ and }L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{E}(\theta)\text{ and }k\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}~.~L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\Gamma[\sigma]\approx\Delta[\sigma^{\prime}]

\mathcal{D}::\frac{\mathcal{E}::\forall~\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L~.~L^{\prime}\vDash\Phi\approx\Phi^{\prime}\quad\mathcal{F}::L\;|\;\mathcal{E}\vDash_{p}^{p}\Gamma\approx\Gamma^{\prime}\quad\mathcal{A}::L\;|\;\Psi;\mathcal{F}\vDash_{\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}}{L\vDash\Phi,u:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\approx\Phi^{\prime},u:(\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash_{i}T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})}

L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}
is defined as

•

\sigma=\sigma_{1},t/u
and
\sigma^{\prime}=\sigma_{1}^{\prime},t^{\prime}/u
,

•

\forall~\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L~.~L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma_{1}\approx\sigma_{1}^{\prime}:\mathcal{E}(\theta)
,

•

Since we know
i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}
, we have
t=t^{\prime}
,

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma[\sigma_{1}]\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}t:T[\sigma_{1}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma^{\prime}[\sigma_{1}^{\prime}]\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}^{\textsc{d}}t:T^{\prime}[\sigma_{1}^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
. In an extension where
i=\textsc{d}
is possible, then we use
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma[\sigma_{1}]\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}t\approx t^{\prime}:T[\sigma_{1}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma^{\prime}[\sigma_{1}^{\prime}]\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}t\approx t^{\prime}:T^{\prime}[\sigma_{1}^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

where
L\;|\;\Psi;\mathcal{F}\vDash_{\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

is defined as given

•

\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L
,

•

\mathcal{B}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{E}(\theta)
,

•

k\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
, and

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\mathcal{F}(\theta,\mathcal{B},k)
,

we have

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}T[\sigma][\delta]\approx T^{\prime}[\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

Now we consider the properties of the new definitions.

Lemma 7.33 (Weakening).

•

If
\mathcal{D}::L\vDash\Phi\approx\Phi^{\prime}
and
\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L
, then

\mathcal{D}^{\prime}::L^{\prime}\vDash\Phi\approx\Phi^{\prime}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}
and
\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L
, then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}^{\prime}
.

Proof.

Immediate by induction on
\mathcal{D}
. We simply use the composition of universe weakenings.
∎

Lemma 7.34 (Reflexive Global Weakening).

If
L\vdash\Phi,\Psi
and
\mathcal{D}::L\vDash\Phi\approx\Phi^{\prime}
, then
L\;|\;\Phi,\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\textsf{wk}_{\Phi}^{|\Psi|}\approx\textsf{wk}_{\Phi}^{|\Psi|}:\mathcal{D}
.

Proof.

We do induction on
\mathcal{D}
.

Case

\mathcal{D}::\frac{\mathcal{E}::\forall~\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L~.~L^{\prime}\vDash\Phi_{1}\approx\Phi_{1}^{\prime}}{L\vDash\Phi_{1},g:\textsf{Ctx}\approx\Phi_{1}^{\prime},g:\textsf{Ctx}}

We have given
\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L
,

(by IH)

\displaystyle L\;|\;\Phi_{1},g:\textsf{Ctx},\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\textsf{wk}_{\Phi_{1}}^{1+|\Psi|}\approx\textsf{wk}_{\Phi_{1}}^{1+|\Psi|}:\mathcal{E}(\theta)

\displaystyle\alpha::L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi_{1},g:\textsf{Ctx},\Psi

(by assumption)

\displaystyle k\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}

(by definition)

\displaystyle L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}g\approx g

In the last line, we know
g
exits in
\Psi^{\prime}
because
\alpha
is a weakening. Hence we have this case.

Case

\mathcal{D}::\frac{\mathcal{E}::\forall~\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L~.~L^{\prime}\vDash\Phi_{1}\approx\Phi_{1}^{\prime}\quad L\;|\;\mathcal{E}\vDash_{p}^{p}\Gamma\approx\Gamma^{\prime}\quad i\in\{\textsc{c},\textsc{d}\}}{L\vDash\Phi_{1},U:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\approx\Phi_{1}^{\prime},U:(\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash_{i}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})}

Subcase

If
i=\textsc{d}
, then given
\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L
and
k\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
, then we
further assume
\alpha::L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi_{1},U:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}),\Psi
and

L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime};\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma
, we should prove

L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime};\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}U^{\delta}\approx U^{\delta^{\prime}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

There is a symmetric proof for
\Gamma^{\prime}
which we omit here. We proceed as follows:

(by escape,
Lemma

7.27
)

\displaystyle L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime};\Delta\vdash_{k}\delta\simeq\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma

(by the neutral types law)

\displaystyle L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime};\Delta\vdash_{k}U^{\delta}\sim U^{\delta^{\prime}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

(by the neutral type case)

\displaystyle L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime};\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}U^{\delta}\approx U^{\delta^{\prime}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

Subcase

If
i=\textsc{c}
, then we know
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}\textsf{id}:\Gamma
. Combining the previous case, we have the goal.

Case

\mathcal{D}::\frac{\mathcal{E}::\forall~\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L~.~L^{\prime}\vDash\Phi_{1}\approx\Phi_{1}^{\prime}\quad\mathcal{F}::L\;|\;\mathcal{E}\vDash_{p}^{p}\Gamma\approx\Gamma^{\prime}\quad\mathcal{A}::L\;|\;\Psi;\mathcal{F}\vDash_{\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}}{L\vDash\Phi_{1},u:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\approx\Phi_{1}^{\prime},u:(\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~^{\prime}$})l}

We first have

\mathcal{B}::L\;|\;\Phi_{1},u:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}),\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\textsf{wk}_{\Phi_{1}}^{1+|\Psi|}\approx\textsf{wk}_{\Phi_{1}}^{1+|\Psi|}:\mathcal{E}(\theta)

by IH.

Then without loss of generality, we should prove

L\;|\;\Phi_{1},u:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}),\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}u^{\textsf{id}}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

which in turn requires

L\;|\;\Phi_{1},u:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}),\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}u^{\textsf{id}}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

Given
\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L
and
k\geq i
,
\alpha::L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi_{1},g:\textsf{Ctx},\Psi
and

\mathcal{C}::L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime};\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma
, we should
prove

L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime};\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}u^{\delta}\approx u^{\delta^{\prime}}:T[\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

We proceed as follows:

(by escape,
Lemma

7.27
)

\displaystyle L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime};\Delta\vdash_{k}\delta\simeq\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma

(as
\mathcal{A}(\theta,\mathcal{B},k,\mathcal{C})
)

\displaystyle L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime};\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}T[\textsf{wk}_{\Phi_{1}}^{1+|\Psi|}][\delta]\approx T[\textsf{wk}_{\Phi_{1}}^{1+|\Psi|}][\delta^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

(omit weakening)

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{\prime}::~

\displaystyle L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime};\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}T[\delta]\approx T[\delta^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

(by the congruence for neutrals law)

\displaystyle L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime};\Delta\vdash_{k}u^{\delta}\sim u^{\delta^{\prime}}:T[\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

(by reflexivity of neutral)

\displaystyle L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime};\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}u^{\delta}\approx u^{\delta^{\prime}}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{C}^{\prime})

∎

Corollary 7.35 (Reflexive Global Identity).

If
L\vdash\Phi
and
\mathcal{D}::L\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\Phi
, then

L\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\textsf{id}_{\Phi}\approx\textsf{id}_{\Phi}:\mathcal{D}
.

Lemma 7.36 (Escape).

•

If
\mathcal{D}::L\vDash\Phi\approx\Phi^{\prime}
, then
L\vdash\Phi
and
L\vdash\Phi^{\prime}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi\vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Phi

and
L\;|\;\Psi\vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Phi^{\prime}
.

Proof.

We do induction on
\mathcal{D}
. We consider one case:

\mathcal{D}::\frac{\mathcal{E}::\forall~\theta::L^{\prime}\Longrightarrow L~.~L^{\prime}\vDash\Phi_{1}\approx\Phi_{1}^{\prime}\quad\mathcal{F}::L\;|\;\mathcal{E}\vDash_{p}^{p}\Gamma\approx\Gamma^{\prime}\quad\mathcal{A}::L\;|\;\Psi;\mathcal{F}\vDash_{\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}}{L\vDash\Phi_{1},u:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\approx\Phi_{1}^{\prime},u:(\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash_{i}T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})}

We proceed as follows:

(by IH)

\displaystyle L\vdash\Phi_{1}\text{ and }L\vdash\Phi_{1}^{\prime}

(by
\mathcal{F}
)

\displaystyle L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\Gamma\approx\Gamma^{\prime}

(applying escape to the previous line)

\displaystyle L\;|\;\Psi\vdash_{\textsc{d}}\Gamma\approx\Gamma^{\prime}

(by
\mathcal{A}
, using reflexive global and local identities)

\displaystyle L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

(by escape)

\displaystyle L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\text{ and }L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{d}}T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

(by definition)

\displaystyle L\vdash\Phi_{1},u:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\text{ and }L\vdash\Phi_{1}^{\prime},u:(\Gamma^{\prime}\vdash_{i}T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})

Then we consider the global substitutions. We know
\sigma=\sigma_{1},t/u
and
\sigma^{\prime}=\sigma_{1}^{\prime},t^{\prime}/u
and
t=t^{\prime}
. Then by IH, we have

L\;|\;\Psi\vdash\sigma_{1}\approx\sigma_{1}^{\prime}:\Phi_{1}\text{ and }L\;|\;\Psi\vdash\sigma_{1}\approx\sigma_{1}^{\prime}:\Phi_{1}^{\prime}

Since we know
i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}
, by passing in a local identity substitution, we have

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma[\sigma_{1}]\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}t\approx t^{\prime}:T[\sigma_{1}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

We then have
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma[\sigma_{1}]\vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T[\sigma_{1}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
by escape. We do it similarly for

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma^{\prime}[\sigma_{1}]\vdash_{\textsc{d}}t\approx t^{\prime}:T^{\prime}[\sigma_{1}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
. By presupposition,
unlifting and analysis using
i
, we can conclude the desired goal.
∎

Lemma 7.37 (Symmetry).

•

If
\mathcal{D}::L\vDash\Phi\approx\Phi^{\prime}
, then
\mathcal{E}::L\vDash\Phi^{\prime}\approx\Phi
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}
, then

L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma^{\prime}\approx\sigma:\mathcal{E}
.

Proof.

We do induction on
\mathcal{D}
. Apply symmetry for previous definitions. ∎

Lemma 7.38 (Right Irrelevance).

If
\mathcal{D}::L\vDash\Phi\approx\Phi^{\prime}
,
\mathcal{E}::L\vDash\Phi\approx\Phi^{\prime\prime}
and

L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{E}
.

Proof.

We do induction on
\mathcal{D}
and then invert
\mathcal{E}
. This lemma is proved similar to previous irrelevance lemma. IHs are sufficient to discharge proof obligations.
∎

Lemma 7.39 (Left Irrelevance).

If
\mathcal{D}::L\vDash\Phi^{\prime}\approx\Phi
,
\mathcal{E}::L\vDash\Phi^{\prime\prime}\approx\Phi
and

L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\mathcal{E}
.

Proof.

Apply symmetry and right irrelevance.
∎

Lemma 7.40 (Transitivity).

•

If
\mathcal{D}_{1}::L\vDash\Phi_{1}\approx\Phi_{2}
and
\mathcal{D}_{2}::L\vDash\Phi_{2}\approx\Phi_{3}
, then
\mathcal{D}_{3}::L\vDash\Phi_{1}\approx\Phi_{3}
.

•

If
\mathcal{E}::L\vDash\Phi_{1}\approx\Phi_{1}
,
L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma_{1}\approx\sigma_{2}:\mathcal{D}_{1}
and

L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma_{2}\approx\sigma_{3}:\mathcal{D}_{2}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma_{1}\approx\sigma_{3}:\mathcal{D}_{3}
.

Proof.

We do induction on
\mathcal{D}_{1}
and then invert

\mathcal{D}_{2}
. The lemma is immediate after use of reflexivity and transitivity of previous
definitions. ∎

7.5.
Logical Relations When
j=\textsc{m}

We are ready for defining the rest of the logical relations. The only cases left are those when
j=\textsc{m}
. In this case, we also know that
i=\textsc{m}
. We first begin with the contextual types for types.

\mathcal{D}::\frac{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}\square(\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi\vdash_{\textsc{m}}\Gamma\quad L\;|\;\Psi\vdash_{\textsc{d}}\Delta\quad L\;|\;\Psi\vdash_{\textsc{d}}\Delta^{\prime}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\simeq\square(\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\Delta\approx\Delta^{\prime}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}}

Then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})
is defined by

•

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}t\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}w:\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}
,

•

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}t^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}w^{\prime}:\square(\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}
,

•

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}w\simeq w^{\prime}:\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}
,

•

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}w\simeq w^{\prime}:\square(\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}
,

•

then we have two auxiliary definitions
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}w\simeq w^{\prime}:\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})
, and

•

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}w\simeq w^{\prime}:\square(\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})
.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\texttt{box}\ T_{1}\simeq\texttt{box}\ T_{1}:\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})}\par\frac{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}\nu\sim\nu^{\prime}:\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\nu\simeq\nu^{\prime}:\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})}

Notice how the relation relies on previous relations where
j=\textsc{d}
. In particular, the local contexts
\Delta
and
\Delta^{\prime}
are related when for layers

\geq\textsc{d}
, so that they are contexts for boxed types. When we relate
t
and
t^{\prime}
, we
follow the routine: we first reduce them to weak head normal forms, and say that the
normal forms are generically equivalent under two types to make symmetry an easy
proof. Then the weak head normal forms are related by the auxiliary relation

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}w\simeq w^{\prime}:\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})
. This relation treats a contextual type as a sum type. There are two possibilities: a normal form of a contextual type is either neutral,
where there is nothing else to do, or contains the code of a type. Due to the static code lemma, we know that these types must be syntactically equal. In this case, if the type is
T_{1}
, we require

L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
. This predicate is what we just defined: it
keeps track of both the syntactic and semantic information about
T_{1}
. In this way, we are sure that
T_{1}
semantically works fine whenever its syntactic
information and/or its semantic information is needed. The same principle is applied to contextual types for terms.

\mathcal{D}::\frac{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}\square(\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{c}}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi\vdash_{\textsc{m}}\Gamma\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{d}}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{d}}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\simeq\square(\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{c}}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\Delta\approx\Delta^{\prime}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T_{1}\approx T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T_{1}^{\prime}\approx T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}}

\mathcal{D}::\frac{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}(g:\textsf{Ctx})\Rightarrow^{l}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}(g:\textsf{Ctx})\Rightarrow^{l}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi\vdash_{\textsc{m}}\Gamma\quad L\;|\;\Psi,g:\textsf{Ctx};\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi,g:\textsf{Ctx};\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}(g:\textsf{Ctx})\Rightarrow^{l}T_{1}\simeq(g:\textsf{Ctx})\Rightarrow^{l}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad\mathcal{E}::\forall~\psi::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime\prime}\Longrightarrow_{i}L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\text{ and }L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\Delta\approx\Delta^{\prime}~.~L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T_{1}[\Delta/g]\approx T_{1}^{\prime}[\Delta^{\prime}/g]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}

The last type is the universe-polymorphic function types. They are special because they parameterize over universe levels. Therefore, a universe-polymorphic function cannot live in any finite universe as it
can be instantiated to any small universe. Our semantics must incorporate this fact and introduce the
\omega
level, which
further requires a transfinite recursion on the universe levels.

\mathcal{D}::\frac{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}\overrightarrow{\ell}\Rightarrow^{l}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\omega$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}\overrightarrow{\ell}\Rightarrow^{l}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\omega$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi\vdash_{\textsc{m}}\Gamma\quad L,\overrightarrow{\ell}\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L,\overrightarrow{\ell}\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}\overrightarrow{\ell}\Rightarrow^{l}T_{1}\simeq\overrightarrow{\ell}\Rightarrow^{l}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\omega$}\quad|\overrightarrow{\ell}|>0\quad\begin{minipage}[345.0pt]$\mathcal{E}::\forall~\psi::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\Longrightarrow_{i}L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\text{ and }|\overrightarrow{\ell}|=|\overrightarrow{l}|=|\overrightarrow{l}^{\prime}|\text{ and }$ \newline
\phantom{10000} $(\forall 0\leq n<|\overrightarrow{l}|~.~L^{\prime}\vdash\overrightarrow{l}(n)\approx\overrightarrow{l}^{\prime}(n):\textsf{Level})~.~L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T_{1}[\overrightarrow{l}/\overrightarrow{\ell}]\approx T_{1}^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{l}^{\prime}/\overrightarrow{\ell}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l[\overrightarrow{l}/\overrightarrow{\ell}]$}$
\end{minipage}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\omega$}}

Then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})
is defined by

•

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}t\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}w:\overrightarrow{\ell}\Rightarrow^{l}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\omega$}
,

•

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}t^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}w^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\ell}\Rightarrow^{l}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\omega$}
,

•

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}w\simeq w^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\ell}\Rightarrow^{l}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\omega$}
,

•

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}w\simeq w^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\ell}\Rightarrow^{l}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\omega$}
, and

•

given

–

\psi::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\Longrightarrow_{i}L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma
,

–

\mathcal{A}::|\overrightarrow{\ell}|=|\overrightarrow{l}|=|\overrightarrow{l}^{\prime}|
, and

–

\mathcal{B}::\forall 0\leq n<|\overrightarrow{l}|~.~L\vdash\overrightarrow{l}(n)\approx\overrightarrow{l}^{\prime}(n):\textsf{Level}
,

then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}w~\$~\overrightarrow{l}\approx w~\$~\overrightarrow{l}^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{E}(\psi,\mathcal{A},\mathcal{B}))

Notice how the premise
\mathcal{E}
changes its universe levels based on different
universes, where exactly transfinite recursion becomes necessary.

Now we have finished defining all definitions, including the Kripke logical relations
for types and terms. The logical relations for local contexts and local substitutions have been given
generally in
Sec.

7.2
. Then we will examine the properties of the logical relations when
i=j=\textsc{m}
. Then we are ready for giving the definitions for the semantic judgments as well as
establish the fundamental theorems. By instantiating the fundamental theorems with syntactic equivalence, we are able to
obtain a few consequence lemmas, which will be subsequently used in our second
instantiation. In the second instantiation of the fundamental theorems, we use the convertibility
checking judgments, from which we derive our final desired the decidability theorem
for convertibility checking.

7.6.
Properties for Logical Relations When
i=j=\textsc{m}

Now we move on to consider the properties of the logical relations at the final
layers, i.e. when
i=j=\textsc{m}
. In this case, we are considering the relations of all possible types among all
possible terms. We first begin with the regular properties, when we will state an important property,
layering restriction, which states how the logical relations are transferred between

i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
when
j=\textsc{m}
. Let us first begin with the simple ones. These properties follow
Sec.

7.3
quite closely as for the types that
exist at both layers
d
and
m
the lemma proceeds in a similar way. The only difference comes in for types only available at layer
m
.

Lemma 7.41 (Weakening).

•

If
\mathcal{D}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and

\psi::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\Longrightarrow_{m}L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma
, then

\mathcal{E}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})
and

\psi::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\Longrightarrow_{m}L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma
, then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t^{\prime}\approx t:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{E})
.

Proof.

Induction on
\mathcal{D}
. For the cases that overlap with
j=\textsc{d}
, they can be ported directly to this
lemma. Therefore, the only cases we need to consider are the new rules. The meta-functions are rather routine as weakenings are built in their
definitions. Let us consider contextual types.

\mathcal{D}::\frac{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}\square(\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{c}}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}\square(\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{c}}T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\simeq\square(\Delta^{\prime}\vdash_{\textsc{c}}T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\Delta\approx\Delta^{\prime}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~0$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}

This case is almost immediate. For judgments like
L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\Delta\approx\Delta^{\prime}
and

L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, we know we can extract from

\psi::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\Longrightarrow_{m}L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma

\alpha::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\Longrightarrow L\;|\;\Psi
. In particular, the local context in
L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
is not
impacted by the weakening. For
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})
, we shall also apply the weakening lemma to
\alpha

to weaken
L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{c}}^{\textsc{d}}t_{1}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.
∎

Lemma 7.42 (Escape).

•

If
\mathcal{D}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T\simeq T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}t\simeq t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}t\simeq t^{\prime}:T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

Proof.

Case analyze
\mathcal{D}
. The lemma holds by construction.
∎

Lemma 7.43 (Reflexivity of Neutral).

If
\mathcal{D}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
,
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}\nu\sim\nu^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}\nu\sim\nu^{\prime}:T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\nu\approx\nu^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})
.

Proof.

Induction on
\mathcal{D}
. We proceed similarly to the counterpart when
j=\textsc{d}
.
∎

Lemma 7.44 (Weak Head Expansion).

•

If
\mathcal{D}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
,
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T_{1}\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}T_{1}^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T_{1}\approx T_{1}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})
,
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}t_{1}\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}t:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{\textsc{m}}t_{1}^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}t^{\prime}:T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t_{1}\approx t_{1}^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})
.

Proof.

Induction on
\mathcal{D}
. Use transitivity of multi-step reductions.
∎

Lemma 7.45 (Symmetry).

•

If
\mathcal{D}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
\mathcal{E}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T^{\prime}\approx T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t^{\prime}\approx t:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{E})
.

Proof.

Induction on
\mathcal{D}
. Symmetry holds by design. The verbose case is the meta-functions for types. Effectively,
\mathcal{E}_{1}
and
\mathcal{E}_{2}
are duplicated to make sure that symmetry can be easily proved.
∎

Lemma 7.46 (Right Irrelevance).

If
\mathcal{D}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
,
\mathcal{E}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{E})
.

Proof.

Induction on
\mathcal{D}
. Again, the overlapping cases for
j\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
can still be ported immediately,
with only layers changed. For contextual types, it is obvious as the logical relations do not depend on the
premises at all. The remaining cases are meta-functions and universe-polymorphic functions. These cases are simpler than that of dependent functions because they only take
simple IH to go through.
∎

Lemma 7.47 (Left Irrelevance).

If
\mathcal{D}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T^{\prime}\approx T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
,
\mathcal{E}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T^{\prime\prime}\approx T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{E})
.

Proof.

Immediate by symmetry and right irrelevance.
∎

Lemma 7.48 (Reflexivity and Transitivity).

•

If
\mathcal{D}_{1}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T_{1}\approx T_{2}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
\mathcal{D}_{2}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T_{2}\approx T_{3}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
\mathcal{D}_{3}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T_{1}\approx T_{3}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
\mathcal{E}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T_{1}\approx T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
,
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t_{1}\approx t_{2}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D}_{1})
and

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t_{2}\approx t_{3}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D}_{2})
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t_{1}\approx t_{3}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D}_{3})
.

•

\mathcal{F}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T_{1}\approx T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t_{1}\approx t_{2}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D}_{1})
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t_{1}\approx t_{1}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{F})
.

Proof.

We do induction on
\mathcal{D}_{1}
and then invert
\mathcal{D}_{2}
. The cases available when
j=\textsc{d}
still can be ported to this lemma. In fact, all remaining cases are simpler than the case for dependent functions. This is because for dependent functions, we must consider reflexivity of related
input arguments, where for all new cases for meta-programming, we only need
transitivity from layer
d
, which has been an established fact at this point. ∎

At last, we must prove one very important lemma which connects the logical relations
when
j
takes different values. We need this lemma to explain the fact that a variable at a lower layer suddenly can
be substituted by a term that is not well-typed at its original layer. This phenomenon typically occurs when we run code from MLTT at layer
m
. In this case, a variable originally only expected to be substituted by a term from
MLTT must also be able to handle a term only available at layer
m
, which might
contain, for example, a recursion principle for code. A similar lemma occurs in
Sec.

2
and
3
, and a dependently typed version
must also be proved here.

Lemma 7.49 (Layering Restriction).

•

If
\mathcal{D}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
\mathcal{E}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

The following two relations are equivalent:

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})\text{ and }L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{E})

The idea of this lemma is that, if we know a type is coming from MLTT only, then it is
possible to regard its term as a term at both layers
d
and
m
. The direction going from
d
to
m
should be intuitive; it resembles the lifting
lemma on the syntactic side. The backward direction, however, might appear counter-intuitive. Yet, if we consider the example we discussed above, then we should consider this lemma
describing a process of bringing a term from
m
back to
d
, performing the
substitution, and then finally lifting the result back to
m
.

Proof.

We do induction on
\mathcal{D}
. Since we know
T
and
T^{\prime}
are related when
j=\textsc{d}
, we do not need to consider the
cases for meta-programming. The most complex case is the function case. In this case, we have premises

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{1}

\displaystyle::(\forall~\psi::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\Longrightarrow_{m}L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma~.~L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}S_{1}\approx S_{2}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$})

\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{2}

\displaystyle::(\forall~\psi::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\Longrightarrow_{m}L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\text{ and }L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}s\approx s^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D}_{1}(\psi))~.~L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}T_{1}[s/x]\approx T_{2}[s^{\prime}/x]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$})

By determinacy, we know that

\displaystyle T\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S_{1}).T_{1}

\displaystyle T^{\prime}\rightsquigarrow^{\ast}\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S_{2}).T_{2}

must be unique. First we show

\mathcal{E}_{1}::(\forall~\psi::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\Longrightarrow_{i}L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma~.~L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}S_{1}\approx S_{2}^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$})

by a simple IH.

Next we must show that given

•

\psi::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\Longrightarrow_{m}L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma
,

•

\mathcal{A}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}s\approx s^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{E}_{1}(\psi))

then

\mathcal{E}_{2}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T_{1}[s/x]\approx T_{2}^{\prime}[s^{\prime}/x]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$})

holds. Notice that the goal is almost applicable for
\mathcal{D}_{2}
except that
\mathcal{A}
does not
satisfy the required premise
L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}s\approx s^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D}_{1}(\psi))
. But this is fine as we apply IH to use the iff in the second statement to obtain the
required premise.

For the second statement, we must establish an iff relation. That boils down to a symmetric proof. Then in this case, we are given

•

\psi::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\Longrightarrow_{m}L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma
,

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\vdash_{i}S_{1}\simeq S_{3}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
,

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\vdash_{i}S_{2}\simeq S_{4}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
,

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta,x:S_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\vdash_{i}T_{1}\simeq T_{3}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$}
, and

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta,x:S_{2}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\vdash_{i}T_{2}\simeq T_{4}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$}
,

and then we have to show the following equivalence:

\mathcal{B}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}s\approx s^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D}_{1}(\psi))

implying

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}(w:\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S_{3}).T_{3})~s\approx(w^{\prime}:\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S_{4}).T_{4})~s^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D}_{2}(\psi,\mathcal{B}))

is equivalent to

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}s\approx s^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{E}_{1}(\psi))

implying

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}(w:\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S_{3}).T_{3})~s\approx(w^{\prime}:\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S_{4}).T_{4})~s^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{E}_{2})

let us consider the inverse direction. In this case, we assume
L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}s\approx s^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{E}_{1}(\psi))
. By IH, we have
L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}s\approx s^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D}_{1}(\psi))
. From this, we further obtain

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}(w:\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S_{3}).T_{3})~s\approx(w^{\prime}:\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S_{4}).T_{4})~s^{\prime}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D}_{2}(\psi,\mathcal{B}))

Another IH is sufficient to establish the goal.
∎

This lemma, when combined with premises like
L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, is
the bridge to reveal the true complication of supporting lifting in
DeLaM
.

We also need a counterpart for local contexts and local substitutions.

Lemma 7.50 (Layering Restriction).

•

If
\mathcal{D}::L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}\Delta\approx\Delta^{\prime}
, then
\mathcal{E}::L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\Delta\approx\Delta^{\prime}
.

•

The following two relations are equivalent:

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}\text{ and }L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\mathcal{E}

Proof.

Induction on
\mathcal{D}
. The step case is very similar to function case above. ∎

7.7.
Semantic Judgments and Fundamental Theorems

After establishing all logical relations and their properties, we are ready for giving
the definitions for semantic judgments and then moving on to proving the fundamental
theorems. The semantic judgments intuitively should say that logical relations are stable under
all substitutions. More concretely, we have

\displaystyle L\Vdash\Psi

\displaystyle:=\forall~L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L~.~L^{\prime}\vDash\Psi[\phi]\approx\Psi[\phi^{\prime}]

L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Phi
is defined as
L\Vdash\Psi
,
L\Vdash\Phi
and
given

•

L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
, and

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma_{1}\approx\sigma_{1}^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
,

then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma[\phi]\circ\sigma_{1}\approx\sigma_{1}[\phi]\circ\sigma_{1}^{\prime}:\Phi[\phi]

We also define

L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\sigma:\Phi:=L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\sigma\approx\sigma:\Phi

L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{i}\Gamma\approx\Delta
where
i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
is defined as
L\Vdash\Psi
and given

•

L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
,

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
, and

•

k\geq i
,

then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]\approx\Delta[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]

We also define

L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{i}\Gamma:=L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{i}\Gamma\approx\Gamma

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
is defined as
L\Vdash\Psi
and
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
and given

•

L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
,

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
,

•

k\geq i
,

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
,

then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}T[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\approx T^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l[\phi]$}

We also define

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}:=L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}T\approx T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
is defined as
L\Vdash\Psi
and
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
and

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
given

•

L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
,

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
,

•

k\geq i
,

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
,

then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}t[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\approx t^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]:T[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l[\phi]$}

We also define

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}:=L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Delta
is defined as
L\Vdash\Psi
and

L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
and
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Delta
given

•

L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
,

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
,

•

k\geq i
,

•

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}\delta_{1}\approx\delta_{1}^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
,

then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}\delta[\phi][\sigma]\circ\delta_{1}\approx\delta^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]\circ\delta_{1}^{\prime}:\Delta[\phi][\sigma]

We also define

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta:\Delta:=L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta:\Delta

Notice that for definitions above, when
k\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}
is possible, we can use the
local substitution lemma and ignore the local substitutions completely. This will be a frequent pattern in the proofs of the semantic rules.

Summarizing the semantic judgments, we shall arriving at our statement of the
fundamental theorems:

Theorem 7.51 (Fundamental).

•

If
L\vdash\Psi
, then
L\Vdash\Psi
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi\vdash_{i}\Gamma
and
i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{i}\Gamma
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi\vdash_{i}\Gamma\approx\Delta
and
i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{i}\Gamma\approx\Delta
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{i}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{i}t:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{i}\delta:\Delta
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta:\Delta
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Delta
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Delta
.

The following lemma is obvious.

Lemma 7.52 (PER).

All semantic judgments are PER.

Lemma 7.53 (Reduction Expansion).

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
,
i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{i}T_{1}\rightsquigarrow T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}T\approx T_{1}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
,
i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\vdash_{i}t_{1}\rightsquigarrow t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
, then

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t_{1}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

Proof.

It is easy to easy due to the weak head expansion lemma for the logical relations
and the stability of reduction under all substitutions.
∎

The escape lemma recovers Kripke logical relations by passing in corresponding
identity substitutions:

Lemma 7.54 (Escape).

•

If
