L\Vdash\Psi
, then
L\vDash\Psi\approx\Psi
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Phi
, then

L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Phi
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{i}\Gamma\approx\Delta
,
i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
and
k\geq i
, then

L\;|\;\Psi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma\approx\Delta
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
k\geq i
, then
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
k\geq i
, then

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}t\approx t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Delta
and
k\geq i
, then

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Delta
.

By chaining escape lemmas, we can obtain that semantic equivalence judgments generic
and syntactic equivalences.

The semantic judgments are stable under substitutions.

Lemma 7.55 (Universe Substitutions).

•

If
L\Vdash\Psi
and
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi:L
, then
L^{\prime}\Vdash\Psi[\phi]
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Phi
and
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
, then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi[\phi]\Vdash\sigma[\phi]\approx\sigma^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}]:\Phi[\phi]
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{i}\Gamma\approx\Delta
,
i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
and
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
, then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi[\phi]\Vdash_{i}\Gamma[\phi]\approx\Delta[\phi^{\prime}]
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
, then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi[\phi];\Gamma[\phi]\Vdash_{i}T[\phi]\approx T^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l[\phi]$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
,
then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi[\phi];\Gamma[\phi]\Vdash_{i}t[\phi]\approx t^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}]:T[\phi]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l[\phi]$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Delta
and
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
, then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi[\phi];\Gamma[\phi]\Vdash_{i}\delta[\phi]\approx\delta^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}]:\Delta[\phi]
.

Proof.

Use composition of universe substitutions.
∎

Lemma 7.56 (Global Substitutions).

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Phi
and
L\;|\;\Psi^{\prime}\Vdash\sigma_{1}\approx\sigma_{1}^{\prime}:\Psi
, then

L\;|\;\Psi^{\prime}\Vdash\sigma\circ\sigma_{1}\approx\sigma^{\prime}\circ\sigma_{1}^{\prime}:\Phi
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{i}\Gamma\approx\Delta
,
i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
and
L\;|\;\Phi\Vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi
, then

L\;|\;\Phi\Vdash_{i}\Gamma[\sigma]\approx\Delta[\sigma^{\prime}]
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L\;|\;\Phi\Vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi
, then

L\;|\;\Phi;\Gamma[\sigma]\Vdash_{i}T[\sigma]\approx T^{\prime}[\sigma^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L\;|\;\Phi\Vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi
,
then

L\;|\;\Phi;\Gamma[\sigma]\Vdash_{i}t[\sigma]\approx t^{\prime}[\sigma^{\prime}]:T[\sigma]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Delta
and
L\;|\;\Phi\Vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi
, then

L\;|\;\Phi;\Gamma[\sigma]\Vdash_{i}\delta[\sigma]\approx\delta^{\prime}[\sigma^{\prime}]:\Delta[\sigma]
.

Proof.

The principle is also to use the composition of global substitutions. We also make use of the commutativity of substitutions, e.g.

t[\phi][\sigma[\phi]]=t[\sigma][\phi]

This equation allows us to swap
\sigma
forwards, which will be frequently used in
this proof.
∎

Lemma 7.57 (Local Substitutions).

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma
, then

L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\Vdash_{i}T[\delta]\approx T^{\prime}[\delta^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and

L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma
, then

L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\Vdash_{i}t[\delta]\approx t^{\prime}[\delta^{\prime}]:T[\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

•

If
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Delta
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma^{\prime}\Vdash_{i}\delta_{1}\approx\delta_{1}^{\prime}:\Gamma
, then

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma^{\prime}\Vdash_{i}\delta\circ\delta_{1}\approx\delta^{\prime}\circ\delta_{1}^{\prime}:\Delta
.

Proof.

We apply the similar technique here. We make use equations similar to below:

\displaystyle t[\phi][\delta[\phi]]=t[\delta][\phi]

\displaystyle t[\sigma][\delta[\sigma]]=t[\delta][\sigma]

These equations will swap
\delta
forwards.
∎

The theorem proceeds by doing induction on the derivations. The proof though is rather verbose due to how the semantic judgments are defined. One pattern that is worth mentioning is that the proof should work “backwards” from
the layers. That is, we should work out the proofs from layer
m
, and then
d
and then
c
and
finally
v
. This pattern makes sense if we consider what information layers contain. The semantics of a term at layer
m
only contains its computational contents at layer

m
. However, for a term at layer
d
, due to lifting, its semantics must explain how this
term computes at both layers
d
and
m
. For a term at layer
c
, in addition to its collective information as a term at layer

d
, it should also has all information about its sub-structures. At last, if a term is at layer
v
, then we know it must be well-formed at layer
c

but also it represents a variable. Thus, due to lifting, information contained at each layer strictly increases as the
layer decreases. To build up information at a smaller layer, we should prove the
fundamental theorems from a higher layer. In the next section, we will start proving the fundamental theorems and make sure that
all syntactically well-formed types and terms at all layers are semantically
well-formed.

7.8.
Proving Fundamental Theorems

To demonstrate the idea described at the end of the previous subsection, let us first
consider the simplest case. We often proceed by first proving the semantic rule for types and then go on and prove
the rules for terms.

Lemma 7.58.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\texttt{Nat}\approx\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}}

Proof.

From
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
, we also know
L\Vdash\Psi
. Now assume
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
, we have to consider all

i\geq\textsc{c}
.

Case
i=\textsc{m}

Then assuming

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\texttt{Nat}\approx\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}

This holds by definition.

Case
i=\textsc{d}

Then assuming some
k\geq\textsc{d}
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\texttt{Nat}\approx\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}

Again, this also holds by definition as we see that
k\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
.

Case
i=\textsc{c}

This is the last case. Assuming some
k\geq\textsc{c}
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\texttt{Nat}\approx\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}

In the previous case of
i=\textsc{d}
, we have given the proof for
k\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
, so
essentially we only have one case
k=\textsc{c}
left. In this case, we apply the local substitution lemma so we do not have to introduce
any local substitutions at all. Looking up the rules in
Sec.

7.4
, we need to show

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}

This is again have been given by the case of
i=\textsc{d}
, modulo converting weakenings
to substitutions. Hence we conclude the proof.

∎

Lemma 7.59.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma\quad L\vdash l\approx l^{\prime}:\textsf{Level}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\texttt{Ty}_{l}\approx\texttt{Ty}_{l^{\prime}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~1+l$}}

Proof.

Similar to the previous lemma.
∎

Lemma 7.60.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\textsf{zero}\approx\textsf{zero}:\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}}

Proof.

From the previous lemma, we obtain
L\Vdash\Psi
,
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
and

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}
. Simulating the previous lemma, assuming
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
, we have to consider all

i\geq\textsc{c}
.

Case
i=\textsc{m}

Then assuming

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\textsf{zero}\approx\textsf{zero}:\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}

This is the same as showing

\mathcal{D}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\texttt{Nat}\approx\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}\text{ and }L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\textsf{zero}\approx\textsf{zero}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})

This is immediate by the congruence law of the generic equivalence and the
definition of the logical relations.

Case
i=\textsc{d}

Then assuming some
k\geq\textsc{d}
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

\mathcal{D}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\texttt{Nat}\approx\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}\text{ and }L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\textsf{zero}\approx\textsf{zero}:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})

Following a similar proof to the case above, we also establish this case knowing

k\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
.

Case
i=\textsc{c}

Assuming some
k\geq\textsc{c}
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\textsf{zero}\approx\textsf{zero}:\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}

The only additional analysis is when
k=\textsc{c}
as other values for
k
have been
considered in the previous case. In this case, we apply local substitution lemma so we only need to prove

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\textsf{zero}:\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}

This clearly has been proven in the previous case.

∎

Lemma 7.61.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\textsf{succ}~t\approx\textsf{succ}~t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}}

Proof.

From
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}
, we also know
L\Vdash\Psi
,

L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
and
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\texttt{Nat}\approx\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}
. Now assume
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
, we have to consider all

i\geq\textsc{c}
.

Case
i=\textsc{m}

Then assuming

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

\mathcal{D}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\texttt{Nat}\approx\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}\text{ and }L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\textsf{succ}~t[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\approx\textsf{succ}~t^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})

From
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}
, we obtain such
\mathcal{D}
and also

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\approx t^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})

From here we obtain
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{i}\textsf{succ}~t\simeq\textsf{succ}~t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}
which leads to our desired
goal.

Case
i=\textsc{d}

Then assuming some
k\geq\textsc{d}
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

\mathcal{D}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\texttt{Nat}\approx\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}\text{ and }L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\textsc{d}}\textsf{succ}~t[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\approx\textsf{succ}~t^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})

We follow the previous case.

Case
i=\textsc{c}

In this case, we only consider the most interesting case of

k=\textsc{c}
. In this case, we apply local substitution lemma, so we know

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}t[\phi][\sigma]:\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}
and we
must prove

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\textsf{succ}~t[\phi][\sigma]:\texttt{Nat}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{zero}$}

This clearly has been given by the previous case. As we have seen in the last few proofs with
k=\textsc{c}
, it is a common pattern that
we use the local substitution lemma to get rid of the local substitution lemma. Then what is left for the proof obligation is given by
i=\textsc{d}
modulo converting
weakenings to substitutions. Essentially, the semantics of layer
c
simply remembers the derivation given by
the semantic rules. For this reason, we will keep cases of
i=\textsc{c}
short.

∎

The semantic rules are pretty sensitive to the orders in which they are proved. To handle
\Pi
types, it is more convenient if we have the rules for contexts
ready.

Lemma 7.62.

\frac{L\Vdash\Psi}{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{i}\cdot\approx\cdot}

Proof.

Immediate.
∎

Lemma 7.63.

\frac{L\Vdash\Psi\quad g:\textsf{Ctx}\in\Psi}{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{i}g\approx g}

Proof.

Now assuming
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
, we have to consider all

i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
. We only consider
i=\textsc{m}
here as the proof for
i=\textsc{d}
is very similar.
We know
g:\textsf{Ctx}\in\Psi[\phi]
as well. Then we have the following after lookup

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\sigma(g)\approx\sigma^{\prime}(g)

We are very close to our goal

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\sigma(g)\approx\sigma^{\prime}(g)

First we obtain
L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}\sigma(g)\approx\sigma^{\prime}(g)
. Then by layering restriction, we have the goal by lifting
d
to
m
.
∎

Lemma 7.64.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{i}\Gamma\approx\Delta\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\vdash l\approx l^{\prime}:\textsf{Level}}{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{i}\Gamma,x:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\approx\Delta,x:T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l^{\prime}$}}

Proof.

Now assuming
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
, we have to consider all

i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
. We only consider
i=\textsc{m}
here as the proof for
i=\textsc{d}
is very similar. We should prove

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\Gamma[\phi][\sigma],x:T[\phi][\sigma]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l[\phi]$}\approx\Delta[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}],x:T^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}]$}

We first obtain

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]\approx\Delta[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]

To obtain the goal, we must show
T[\phi][\sigma]\approx T^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]
is
stable under local substitutions. This is immediate by the semantic judgment, after converting weakenings into
universe and global substitutions.
∎

Lemma 7.65.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma\quad\text{$\Gamma$ ends with $\cdot$}\quad|\Gamma|=k^{\prime}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\cdot^{k^{\prime}}\approx\cdot^{k^{\prime}}:\cdot}

Proof.

From
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
, we also know
L\Vdash\Psi
. Assuming

L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
,
L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
. We should consider all possible
i
.

Case
i=\textsc{m}

Then assuming

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

\mathcal{D}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\cdot\approx\cdot\text{ and }L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\cdot^{k^{\prime}}\circ\delta\approx\cdot^{k^{\prime}}\circ\delta^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}

\mathcal{D}
is immediate. Now we should consider the composition. We know

{\cdot^{k^{\prime}}\circ\delta}={\cdot^{k^{\prime}}\circ\delta^{\prime}}=\cdot_{\widecheck{\delta}}^{\widehat{\delta}}

We then have the goal by definition.

Case
i=\textsc{d}

Similar.

Case
i=\textsc{c}

By the local substitution lemma and the rule in

Sec.

7.4
, we conclude this case by repeating the previous case.

Case
i=\textsc{v}

Similar.

∎

Lemma 7.66.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma\quad g:\textsf{Ctx}\in\Psi\quad\text{$\Gamma$ ends with $g$}\quad|\Gamma|=k^{\prime}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\cdot_{g}^{k^{\prime}}\approx\cdot_{g}^{k^{\prime}}:\cdot}

Proof.

Similar to the previous lemma. We will need to do a case analysis on the result of lookup of
g
, but otherwise the
result is straightforward.
∎

Lemma 7.67.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma\quad g:\textsf{Ctx}\in\Psi\quad\text{$\Gamma$ ends with $g$}\quad|\Gamma|=k^{\prime}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\textsf{wk}_{g}^{k^{\prime}}\approx\textsf{wk}_{g}^{k^{\prime}}:g}

Proof.

From
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
, we also know
L\Vdash\Psi
. The semantic well-formedness for
g
is established by a previous lemma.

Now we assume
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
,

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
. We should consider all possible
i
.

Case
i=\textsc{m}

Then assuming

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

\mathcal{D}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\sigma(g)\approx\sigma^{\prime}(g)\text{ and }L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\textsf{wk}_{g}^{k^{\prime}}[\sigma]\circ\delta\approx\textsf{wk}_{g}^{k^{\prime}}[\sigma^{\prime}]\circ\delta^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}

\mathcal{D}
is obtained by looking up
g
in
\sigma
, from which we get

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}\sigma(g)\approx\sigma^{\prime}(g)

We have
\mathcal{D}
by layering restriction.

For composition, we have

\displaystyle\textsf{wk}_{g}^{k^{\prime}}[\sigma]\circ\delta=\textsf{wk}_{\sigma(g)}^{k^{\prime}}\circ\delta

\displaystyle\textsf{wk}_{g}^{k^{\prime}}[\sigma^{\prime}]\circ\delta^{\prime}=\textsf{wk}_{\sigma^{\prime}(g)}^{k^{\prime}}\circ\delta^{\prime}

Effectively, this is the same as popping off
k^{\prime}
terms from
\delta
and

\delta^{\prime}
simultaneously. We have the goal by irrelevance.

Case
i=\textsc{d}

Similar.

Case
i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}

Similarly, we use the previous case.

∎

Lemma 7.68.

\frac{L\vdash l:\textsf{Level}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Delta\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Delta\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T[\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta,t/x\approx\delta^{\prime},t^{\prime}/x:\Delta,x:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}

Proof.

From
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Delta
we have
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
,

L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Delta
and
L\Vdash\Psi
. Then from a previous lemma, we further have

L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Delta,x:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
.

Now we assume
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
,

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
. We should consider all possible
i
.

Case
i=\textsc{m}

Then assuming

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta_{1}\approx\delta_{1}^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

–

\mathcal{D}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}(\Delta,x:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})[\phi][\sigma]\approx(\Delta,x:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]
and

–

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}(\delta,t/x)[\phi][\sigma]\circ\delta_{1}\approx(\delta^{\prime},t^{\prime}/x)[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]\circ\delta_{1}^{\prime}:\mathcal{D}
.

We expand the composition:

\displaystyle(\delta,t/x)[\phi][\sigma]\circ\delta_{1}

\displaystyle=(\delta[\phi][\sigma]\circ\delta_{1}),(t[\phi][\sigma][\delta_{1}])/x

\displaystyle(\delta^{\prime},t^{\prime}/x)[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]\circ\delta_{1}^{\prime}

\displaystyle=(\delta^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]\circ\delta_{1}^{\prime}),(t^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta_{1}^{\prime}])/x

We can conclude the goal by using
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Delta,x:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
,

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T[\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}
and irrelevance.

Case
i=\textsc{d}

Similar.

Case
i=\textsc{c}

Similar to the previous pattern, we apply the local substitution
lemma and use the previous case to discharge the obligations.

Case
i=\textsc{v}

Similar.

∎

Lemma 7.69.

\frac{}{L\Vdash\cdot}\par\frac{L\Vdash\Psi}{L\Vdash\Psi,g:\textsf{Ctx}}\par\frac{L\Vdash\Psi\quad L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\textsc{d}}\Gamma\quad\quad L\vdash l:\textsf{Level}\quad i\in\{\textsc{c},\textsc{d}\}}{L\Vdash\Psi,U:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})}\par\frac{L\Vdash\Psi\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad\quad L\vdash l:\textsf{Level}\quad i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}}{L\Vdash\Psi,u:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})}

Proof.

Immediate. We take advantage of the fact that a universe weakening is a special universe
substitution. ∎

Lemma 7.70.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma\quad x:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\in\Gamma}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}x\approx x:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}

Proof.

From
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
, we also know
L\Vdash\Psi
.

To construct the semantic judgment for type
T
, we first assuming

L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
,
L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
and
some
k\geq i
. We have

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]\approx\Gamma[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]

Our goal is to construct

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}T[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\approx T^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l[\phi]$}

with further assuming

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
. This is done by doing induction on
x:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\in\Gamma
.

Then we consider the term. Since it is the variable case,
i
can take all four layers.

Case
i=\textsc{m}

Then assuming

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

\mathcal{D}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\approx T^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l[\phi]$}\text{ and }L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta(x)\approx\delta^{\prime}(x):\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})

We proceed by doing induction on
x:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\in\Gamma
. We weaken the universe and global contexts to obtain the goal.

Case
i=\textsc{d}

This case works similarly at different layers. We omit it here.

Case
i=\textsc{c}

In this case, we consider
k=\textsc{c}
and apply the local
substitution lemma. Based on the rule in
Sec.

7.4
and the previous case, we have the
goal.

Case
i=\textsc{v}

This case makes use of the entire previous case and also in
addition must prove the same for
k=\textsc{v}
. But this is virtually identical to the previous case.

∎

Combining the semantic rules for local substitutions, we derive that

Corollary 7.71 (Local Weakening Substitutions).

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma,\Delta\Vdash_{i}\textsf{wk}_{\Gamma}^{|\Delta|}:\Gamma

Lemma 7.72.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma\quad u:(\Delta\vdash_{i^{\prime}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\in\Psi\quad i^{\prime}\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}\quad i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c},\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}\quad i^{\prime}\leq i\quad\mathcal{A}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Delta}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}u^{\delta}\approx u^{\delta^{\prime}}:T[\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}

Proof.

From
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
, we also know
L\Vdash\Psi
.

To construct the semantic judgment for type
T
, we first assuming

L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
,
L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
and
some
k\geq i
, and then

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}\delta_{1}\approx\delta_{1}^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
. We have

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]\approx\Gamma[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]

Our goal is to construct

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{k}^{\Uparrow(i)}T[\phi][\sigma][\delta[\phi][\sigma]\circ\delta_{1}]\approx T[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]\circ\delta_{1}^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l[\phi]$}

We obtain this by looking up
L^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\Psi[\phi]
using

u:(\Delta\vdash_{i^{\prime}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\in\Psi
, and use the invariant that
T
is stable under
global and local substitutions.

Then we consider the term.

Case
i=\textsc{m}

Then assuming

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta_{1}\approx\delta_{1}^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must show

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\sigma(u)[\delta[\phi][\sigma]\circ\delta_{1}]\approx\sigma^{\prime}(u)[\delta^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]\circ\delta_{1}^{\prime}]:T[\phi][\sigma][\delta[\phi][\sigma]\circ\delta_{1}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l[\phi]$}

Looking up
L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
, we know that

\mathcal{B}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta[\phi][\sigma]\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq i^{\prime}}^{\textsc{d}}\sigma(u)\approx\sigma^{\prime}(u):T[\phi][\sigma]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\Delta[\phi][\sigma]

We also know the following from
\mathcal{A}

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta[\phi][\sigma]\circ\delta_{1}\approx\delta^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]\circ\delta_{1}^{\prime}:\Delta[\phi][\sigma]

Therefore, we can apply layering restriction and obtain

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}\delta[\phi][\sigma]\circ\delta_{1}\approx\delta^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]\circ\delta_{1}^{\prime}:\Delta[\phi][\sigma]

This is because we are sure
\Delta[\phi][\sigma]
only contains types from MLTT.

We want to lift
\sigma(u)
and
\sigma^{\prime}(u)
to
m
, so we instantiate
\mathcal{B}
with
the related local substitutions above and obtain

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{d}}\sigma(u)[\delta[\phi][\sigma]\circ\delta_{1}]\approx\sigma^{\prime}(u)[\delta^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}]\circ\delta_{1}^{\prime}]:T[\phi][\sigma][\delta[\phi][\sigma]\circ\delta_{1}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l[\phi]$}

The goal is achieved by another layering restriction.

Case
i=\textsc{d}

Similar to the previous case except that there is no need for
layering restriction, as we are evaluating terms right inside of MLTT and
therefore no lifting occurs.

Case
i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}

In this case, we also follow similar footsteps as the
results of looking up global substitutions must be stable under local
substitutions.

∎

Lemma 7.73.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma\quad U:(\Delta\vdash_{i^{\prime}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})\in\Psi\quad i^{\prime}\in\{\textsc{c},\textsc{d}\}\quad i^{\prime}\leq i\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Delta}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}U^{\delta}\approx U^{\delta^{\prime}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}

Proof.

Similar to above, but simpler. Use layering restriction as well when
i=\textsc{m}
.
∎

Lemma 7.74.

\frac{L\Vdash\Psi}{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\cdot\approx\cdot:\cdot}\par\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Phi\quad L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\textsc{d}}\Gamma\approx\Delta}{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\sigma,\Gamma/g\approx\sigma^{\prime},\Delta/g:\Phi,g:\textsf{Ctx}}

Proof.

Immediate.
∎

Lemma 7.75.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Phi\quad L\;|\;\Phi;\Gamma\Vdash_{\textsc{d}}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\quad L\vdash l:\textsf{Level}\quad i\in\{\textsc{v},\textsc{c}\}\quad\mathcal{A}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma[\sigma]\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T[\sigma]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\sigma,t/u\approx\sigma^{\prime},t^{\prime}/u:\Phi,u:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})}

Proof.

Assuming
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma_{1}\approx\sigma_{1}^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
, we have to show

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Psi^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma[\phi]\circ\sigma_{1},t[\phi][\sigma_{1}]/u\approx\sigma^{\prime}[\phi]\circ\sigma_{1}^{\prime},t[\phi][\sigma_{1}^{\prime}]/u:(\Phi,u:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}))[\phi]

Our goal is to show, without loss of generality,

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma[\sigma[\phi]\circ\sigma_{1}]\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq i}^{\textsc{d}}t[\phi][\sigma_{1}]\approx t^{\prime}[\phi][\sigma_{1}^{\prime}]:T[\sigma[\phi]\circ\sigma_{1}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}

This is given by
\mathcal{A}
, modulo converting weakenings to substitutions. ∎

Lemma 7.76.

\frac{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Phi\quad L\;|\;\Phi\Vdash_{\textsc{d}}\Gamma\quad L\vdash l:\textsf{Level}\quad i\in\{\textsc{c},\textsc{d}\}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma[\sigma]\Vdash_{i}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}{L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash\sigma,T/U\approx\sigma^{\prime},T^{\prime}/U:\Phi,u:(\Gamma\vdash_{i}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$})}

Proof.

Similar to the previous case but simpler.
∎

Corollary 7.77 (Global Weakening Substitutions).

L\;|\;\Psi,\Phi\Vdash\textsf{wk}_{\Psi}^{|\Phi|}:\Psi

Lemma 7.78.

\frac{L\vdash l_{1}\approx l_{3}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\vdash l_{2}\approx l_{4}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}S\approx S^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma,x:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\Vdash_{i}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).T\approx\Pi^{l_{3},l_{4}}(x:S^{\prime}).T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}\sqcup l_{2}$}}

Proof.

From
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}S\approx S^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}
, we can conclude
L\Vdash\Psi
and
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
. Now assuming
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
, we have to consider all

i\geq\textsc{c}
.

Case
i=\textsc{m}

Then assuming

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).T[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\approx\Pi^{l_{3},l_{4}}(x:S^{\prime}).T^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~(l_{1}\sqcup l_{2})[\phi]$}

From escape lemmas, we are able to establish the reduction premises, typing
premises and the generic equivalence. We then only focus on the semantic premises. First, from
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}S\approx S^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}
, we obtain

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}S[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\approx S^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}[\phi]$}

Then we further assume

\psi::L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Phi^{\prime};\Delta^{\prime}\Longrightarrow_{m}L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta
and

L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Phi^{\prime};\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}s\approx s^{\prime}:S[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}[\phi]$}
,
we should prove

L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Phi^{\prime};\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}T[\phi][\sigma][\delta,s/x]\approx T^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime},s^{\prime}/x]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}[\phi]$}

We are almost there, as long as we can provide

L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Phi^{\prime};\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta,s/x\approx\delta^{\prime},s^{\prime}/x:(\Gamma,x:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$})[\phi][\sigma]

To prove these two local substitutions are related, we are interested in showing
L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma,x:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\approx\Gamma,x:S^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{3}$}
, but
this is immediate from a previous lemma.

Case
i=\textsc{d}

This case follows similarly to the previous case. It must range over
k\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
so a similar reasoning must be repeated twice.

Case
i=\textsc{c}

This case is much simpler by using the local substitution lemma to remove the need
to assume another local substitution. Then we can simply apply identity local substitutions to all premises and use the
previous case to conclude

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\geq\textsc{d}}^{\textsc{d}}\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~(l_{1}\sqcup l_{2})[\phi]$}

If we introduce another local substitution, then we must reason about extending a
local variable to an arbitrary local substitution, which is quite verbose and
unnecessary.

∎

Lemma 7.79.

\frac{L\vdash l\approx l^{\prime}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{l}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~1+l$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\texttt{El}^{l}~t\approx\texttt{El}^{l^{\prime}}~t^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}}

Proof.

We use the fact that
\texttt{Ty}_{l}
reduces only to itself and therefore it is only
possible to expand
t\approx t^{\prime}
to the universe case. ∎

Lemma 7.80.

\frac{L\vdash l_{1}\approx l_{3}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\vdash l_{2}\approx l_{4}:\textsf{Level}\quad\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}s\approx s^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{l_{1}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~1+l_{1}$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma,x:\texttt{El}^{l_{1}}~s\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{l_{2}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~1+l_{2}$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:s).t\approx\Pi^{l_{3},l_{4}}(x:s^{\prime}).t^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{l_{1}\sqcup l_{2}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~\textsf{succ}~(l_{1}\sqcup l_{2})$}}

Proof.

Very similar to the previous proof. We use the previous lemma and know that

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\texttt{El}^{l_{1}}~s\approx\texttt{El}^{l_{3}}~s^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}

We do the same for
t\approx t^{\prime}
. This gives us

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:\texttt{El}^{l_{1}}~s).\texttt{El}^{l_{2}}~t\approx\Pi^{l_{3},l_{4}}(x:\texttt{El}^{l_{3}}~s^{\prime}).\texttt{El}^{l_{4}}~t^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}\sqcup l_{2}$}

When
i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
, we use

\displaystyle\texttt{El}^{l_{1}\sqcup l_{2}}~\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:s).t

\displaystyle\rightsquigarrow{\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:\texttt{El}^{l_{1}}~s).\texttt{El}^{l_{2}}~t}

\displaystyle\texttt{El}^{l_{3}\sqcup l_{4}}~\Pi^{l_{3},l_{4}}(x:s^{\prime}).t^{\prime}

\displaystyle\rightsquigarrow{\Pi^{l_{3},l_{4}}(x:\texttt{El}^{l_{3}}~s^{\prime}).\texttt{El}^{l_{4}}~t^{\prime}}

We have the goal using reduction expansion.
∎

Lemma 7.81.

\frac{L\vdash l:\textsf{Level}\quad L\vdash l^{\prime}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}s:\texttt{Ty}_{l}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~1+l$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma,x:\texttt{El}^{l}~s\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l$}\Vdash_{i}t:\texttt{Ty}_{l^{\prime}}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~1+l^{\prime}$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\Pi^{l,l^{\prime}}(x:\texttt{El}^{l}~s).\texttt{El}^{l^{\prime}}~t\approx\texttt{El}^{l\sqcup l^{\prime}}~\Pi^{l,l^{\prime}}(x:s).t\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l\sqcup l^{\prime}$}}

Proof.

Here
i\in\{\textsc{d},\textsc{m}\}
. The proof is similar to the previous lemma except that we only use reduction
expansion on one side.
∎

Lemma 7.82.

\frac{L\vdash l_{1}\approx l_{3}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\vdash l_{2}\approx l_{4}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}S\approx S^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma,x:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\lambda^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).t\approx\lambda^{l_{3},l_{4}}(x:S^{\prime}).t^{\prime}:\Pi^{l,l^{\prime}}(x:S).T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}\sqcup l_{2}$}}

Proof.

From
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}S\approx S^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}
, we can conclude
L\Vdash\Psi
and

L\;|\;\Psi\Vdash_{\Uparrow(i)}\Gamma
. The premise
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma,x:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$}
also gives
us
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma,x:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\Vdash_{i}T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$}
. Combining symmetry, transitivity and the previous lemma, we have

L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}\sqcup l_{2}$}

Now assuming
L^{\prime}\vdash\phi\approx\phi^{\prime}:L
and

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}\sigma\approx\sigma^{\prime}:\Psi[\phi]
, we have to consider all

i\geq\textsc{c}
.

Case
i=\textsc{m}

Then assuming

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta\approx\delta^{\prime}:\Gamma[\phi][\sigma]
, we must
show

–

\mathcal{D}::L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).T[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\approx\Pi^{l_{3},l_{4}}(x:S).T[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~(l_{1}\sqcup l_{2})[\phi]$}
, and

–

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\lambda^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).t[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\approx\lambda^{l_{3},l_{4}}(x:S^{\prime}).t^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]:\texttt{El}(\mathcal{D})
.

To obtain the goal we shall proceed in two steps. First, we obtain

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta,x:S[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}[\phi]$}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta,x/x\approx\delta^{\prime},x/x:(\Gamma,x:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$})[\phi][\sigma]

. Giving it to
L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma,x:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$}
, we
have

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta,x:S[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}[\phi]$}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t[\phi][\sigma][\delta,x/x]\approx t^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime},x/x]:T[\phi][\sigma][\delta,x/x]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}[\phi]$}

A further escape gives us

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta,x:S[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}[\phi]$}\vdash_{\textsc{m}}t[\phi][\sigma][\delta,x/x]\simeq t^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime},x/x]:T[\phi][\sigma][\delta,x/x]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}[\phi]$}

From this, we conclude

L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta\vdash_{\textsc{m}}(\lambda^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).t)[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\simeq(\lambda^{l_{3},l_{4}}(x:S^{\prime}).t^{\prime})[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime}]:T[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}[\phi]$}

modulo the law of weak head closure.

In the second step, we assume

\psi::L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Phi^{\prime};\Delta^{\prime}\Longrightarrow_{m}L^{\prime}\;|\;\Phi;\Delta
and

L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Phi^{\prime};\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}s\approx s^{\prime}:S[\phi][\sigma][\delta]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}[\phi]$}
,
we should prove

L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Phi^{\prime};\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}t[\phi][\sigma][\delta,s/x]\approx t^{\prime}[\phi^{\prime}][\sigma^{\prime}][\delta^{\prime},s^{\prime}/x]:T[\phi][\sigma][\delta,s/x]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}[\phi]$}

then we have the goal modulo weak head expansion. We are almost there, as long as we can provide

L^{\prime\prime}\;|\;\Phi^{\prime};\Delta^{\prime}\mathrel{\raisebox{0.01pt}{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}\hskip 1.49994pt{\rule{0.45pt}{6.88889pt}}{\hskip 1.00006pt\vDash}}_{\textsc{m}}^{\textsc{m}}\delta,s/x\approx\delta^{\prime},s^{\prime}/x:(\Gamma,x:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$})[\phi][\sigma]

But in the previous lemma, we have seen it obvious.

Lemma 7.83.

\frac{L\vdash l_{1}\approx l_{3}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\vdash l_{2}\approx l_{4}:\textsf{Level}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}S\approx S^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma,x:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}\Vdash_{i}T\approx T^{\prime}\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$}\quad\mathcal{A}::L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}t\approx t^{\prime}:\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).T\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}\sqcup l_{2}$}\quad L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}s\approx s^{\prime}:S\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{1}$}}{L\;|\;\Psi;\Gamma\Vdash_{i}(t:\Pi^{l_{1},l_{2}}(x:S).T)~s\approx(t^{\prime}:\Pi^{l_{3},l_{4}}(x:S^{\prime}).T^{\prime})~s^{\prime}:T[s/x]\scalebox{0.75}{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}$\,@~l_{2}$}}

7.71
.

i\geq\textsc{c}
.

i=\textsc{m}
